- **ccc-hermes**: Optional bridge between Claude Code and a local Hermes agent gateway: chat channels can wake a Claude Code session, and Claude Code can post progress or accept escalations over MCP. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/tree/HEAD/commander/cowork-plugin/skills/ccc-hermes) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **ccc-hook-wiring**: Use when modifying `src/hooks/template.ts`, `src/hooks/from-hook.ts`, or adding hook-driven features. Captures what this repo learned about Claude Code's hook lifecycle — especially the non-obvious gap in `PreToolUse` / `PostToolUse` coverage for the `Agent`/`Task` tool, and the idempotency + non-breaking contracts hooks must uphold. ([Elad73/claude-command-central](https://github.com/Elad73/claude-command-central/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ccc-hook-wiring))
